  You control a creature other than Bloodghast that is untapped and that has a +1/+1 counter on it.

## Combo 4772-4968-6593

Produces: Infinite damage; Infinite +1/+1 counters on a creature
Start: Gatta and Luzzu (hand); Doubling Season (battlefield); Deathbringer Thoctar (battlefield: with a +1/+1 counter on it)

  When Gatta and Luzzu enters the battlefield, it triggers, targeting Deathbringer Thoctar.
  Activate Deathbringer Thoctar by removing a +1/+1 counter from it, targeting itself, but instead putting two +1/+1 counters on it due to Gatta and Luzzu and Doubling Season.

  Repeat from step 3.
Notes: You can forego step 4 to simply put infinite +1/+1 counters on Deathbringer Thoctar if desired.

## Combo 483-1116-3240-5670
Cards: Sek'Kuar, Deathkeeper + Warren Soultrader + Nether Traitor + Cruel Celebrant
Color identity: BRGW | Bracket: Exhibition | Popularity: 0 | Commander: legal
Produces: Infinite artifact ETB; Infinite artifact LTB; Infinite artifact sacrifice triggers; Infinite artifact tokens; Infinite creature LTB; Infinite creature ETB; Infinite colored mana; Infinite creature sacrifice triggers; Infinite death triggers; Infinite lifegain triggers; Infinite lifeloss; Infinite Treasure tokens
Start: Sek'Kuar, Deathkeeper (battlefield); Warren Soultrader (battlefield); Nether Traitor (battlefield); Cruel Celebrant (battlefield)

  When Nether Traitor dies, Cruel Celebrant and Sek'Kuar trigger.

  Activate the Treasure by tapping and sacrificing it, adding {B}.
  Activate Warren Soultrader by paying 1 life and sacrificing the Graveborn.
  When the Graveborn dies, Cruel Celebrant and Nether Traitor trigger.
  Resolve the Cruel Celebrant trigger, causing you to gain 1 life and each opponent to lose 1 life.
  Resolve the Nether Traitor trigger, causing you to pay {B} to return Nether Traitor from your graveyard to the battlefield.
  Resolve the Warren Soultrader ability, creating a Treasure token.
  Repeat.

  Repeat.

Produces: Infinite +1/+1 counters on a creature; Infinite artifact ETB; Infinite artifact LTB; Infinite artifact sacrifice triggers; Infinite creature ETB; Infinite creature LTB; Infinite creature sacrifice triggers; Infinite death triggers; Infinite landfall triggers
Start: Toph, the First Metalbender (battlefield); Haru, Hidden Talent (battlefield); Conspiracy (battlefield); Phantom Train (battlefield)
Prerequisites: Conspiracy has "Ally" chosen.
  You have an artifact creature in hand with the mana to cast it.
Steps: Cast any artifact creature by paying its mana cost.
  When the artifact creature enters as an Ally due to Conspiracy, Haru triggers, earthbending the artifact creature, as it's a land due to Toph.
  Activate Phantom Train by sacrificing the artifact creature.
  When the creature dies, Haru triggers, returning the creature from your graveyard to the battlefield.
  When the creature enters as an Ally due to Conspiracy, Haru triggers, earthbending the creature, as it's a land due to Toph.
  Resolve the Phantom Train ability, putting a +1/+1 counter on Phantom Train and causing it to become a creature until end of turn.
  Repeat from step 3.

  Resolve a copy of Early Harvest, untapping lands you control.
  Activate those lands by tapping them, adding mana.

  Activate those lands by tapping them, adding mana.

  Resolve Pyromancer Ascension's first abiliity, putting a redundant quest counter on it if desired.
  Resolve Pyromancer Ascension's second ability, copying Regrowth and targeting Emeritus of Abundance with the copy.

Produces: Infinite artifact ETB; Infinite artifact LTB; Infinite artifact sacrifice triggers; Infinite creature ETB; Infinite creature LTB; Infinite creature sacrifice triggers; Infinite death triggers; Infinite landfall triggers; Infinite scry 1
Requires: Creature with ETB: Earthbend (hand)
Start: Toph, the First Metalbender (battlefield); Viscera Seer (battlefield); Biotransference (battlefield)
Prerequisites: You have enough mana to cast the earthbend creature.
Steps: Cast the earthbend creature by paying its mana cost.
  When the creature enters, it triggers, earthbending the creature, as it's an artifact due to Biotransference, thus making it also a land due to Toph.
  Activate Viscera Seer by sacrificing the earthbent creature.
  When the creature dies, it triggers, returning it from your graveyard to the battlefield tapped.
  When the creature enters, it triggers, earthbending the creature, as it's an artifact due to Biotransference, thus making it also a land due to Toph.
  Resolve the Viscera Seer ability, causing you to scry 1.
  Repeat from step 3.
Notes: Depending on the earthbend creature used, you may receive additional results. There may also be needed prerequisites that are specific for each card (such as Bumi, King of Three Trials which requires a Lesson in your graveyard).

## Combo 549-2438-6871--183

Color identity: BRGW | Bracket: Exhibition | Popularity: 0 | Commander: legal | Mana needed: {0} plus the cost of the creature that earthbends

Requires: Creature that earthbends on entering (hand)
Start: Toph, the First Metalbender (battlefield); Biotransference (battlefield); Carrion Feeder (battlefield)
Prerequisites: You have at least 2 life.
Steps: Cast the earthbending creature by paying its mana cost.
  When you cast that spell, you lose 1 life and create a 2/2 artifact creature token.
  The earthbending creature enters as an artifact land creature due to Biotransference and Toph.
  When that creature enters, it earthbends, causing itself to become a land creature with haste, putting at least one +1/+1 counter on itself, and creating a delayed triggered ability.
  Activate Carrion Feeder by sacrificing that creature.
  When that creature dies, the delayed ability from step 4 triggers, causing it to return to the battlefield tapped as an artifact land creature.
  Repeat from step 3 any number of times.
  Resolve all activated abilities from Carrion Feeder.

Start: Hogaak, Arisen Necropolis (battlefield); Altar of Dementia (battlefield); Totentanz, Swarm Piper (battlefield); Ratadrabik of Urborg (battlefield)

  Repeat.

Produces: Near-infinite damage; Infinite self-discard triggers; Near-infinite creature ETB; Infinite draw triggers; Near-infinite creature LTB; Infinite rummaging; Near-infinite death triggers; Near-infinite creature sacrifice triggers
Start: Zombie Infestation (battlefield); Queen Allenal of Ruadach (battlefield); Goblin Bombardment (battlefield); Liliana, Dreadhorde General (battlefield)
Prerequisites: You have at least two cards in hand.
Steps: Activate Zombie Infestation by discarding two cards, creating a 2/2 Zombie creature token and a 1/1 Soldier creature token.
  Activate Goblin Bombardment twice by sacrificing the Zombie and Soldier.
  When the Zombie and Soldier die, Liliana, Dreadhorde General triggers twice, causing you to draw two cards.
  Resolve both Goblin Bombardment abilities.
  Repeat.

  Resolve the Etali trigger from step 2, exiling cards from the top of each library until each player has exiled a nonland card, and causing you to cast any number of those cards without paying its mana cost.
  For each card cast, Toph's second ability triggers once.
  Resolve the first Toph trigger, earthbending Etali.
  Resolve any remaining triggers, earthbending any other lands you desire. If you do not want to earthbend other lands, target Etali with all remaining triggers.
  Activate Shilgengar by sacrificing Etali.
  When Etali dies, Toph and Yedora trigger.
  Resolve the Yedora trigger, choosing not to return Etali.
  Resolve the Toph trigger, returning Etali from your graveyard to the battlefield tapped.
  Repeat from step 2.
  Resolve all Shilgengar abilities from step 11.

  Cast Victimize by paying {2}{B} and sacrificing another creature, returning Eternal Witness and Priest of Gix from your graveyard to the battlefield.
  When Priest of Gix and Eternal Witness enter, they each trigger as well as Quintorius.
  Resolve the Priest of Gix trigger, adding {B}{B}{B}.
  Resolve the Quintorius trigger, creating a creature token
  Resolve the Eternal Witness trigger, returning Victimize from your graveyard to your hand.
  Quintorius triggers, creating a creature token
  Repeat.

## Combo 62-280-833-3827
Cards: Soul of Windgrace + Waves of Aggression + Savage Ventmaw + Golgari Rot Farm
Color identity: BRGW | Bracket: Exhibition | Popularity: 0 | Commander: legal
Produces: Infinite combat phases; Infinite landfall triggers; Infinite magecraft triggers; Infinite self-discard triggers; Infinite storm count; Infinite untap of most creatures you control; Infinite green mana; Infinite red mana
Start: Soul of Windgrace (battlefield); Waves of Aggression (graveyard); Savage Ventmaw (battlefield); Golgari Rot Farm (graveyard)
Prerequisites: Soul of Windgrace can attack without taking damage.
  Savage Ventmaw can attack without taking damage.
Steps: Declare Savage Ventmaw and Soul of Windgrace as attackers.
  When they attack, Savage Ventmaw and Soul of Windgrace trigger.
  Resolve the Savage Ventmaw trigger, adding six mana that you will not lose this turn as steps and phases end.
  Resolve the Soul of Windgrace trigger, returning Golgari Rot Farm from your graveyard to the battlefield tapped.
  When Golgari Rot Farm enters, it triggers, returning itself from the battlefield to your hand.
  In your next main phase, cast Waves of Aggression from your graveyard for its retrace cost by paying {3}{R/W}{R/W} and discarding Golgari Rot Farm, causing you to untap all creatures that attacked this turn, and to get an additional combat and main phase after this one.
  Repeat.

Color identity: BRGW | Bracket: Exhibition | Popularity: 0 | Commander: legal
Produces: Infinite creature LTB; Infinite creature ETB; Infinite creature sacrifice triggers; Infinite death triggers; Infinite self-discard triggers; Infinite +1/+1 counters on a creature
Start: Disa the Restless (battlefield); Enduring Renewal (battlefield); Circling Vultures (hand); Ashes of the Fallen (battlefield); Bloodflow Connoisseur (battlefield)
Prerequisites: "Lhurgoyf" chosen with Ashes of the Fallen.
Steps: Discard Circling Vultures from your hand using its static ability.
  Because Circling Vultures is a Lhurgoyf due to Ashes of the Fallen, Disa the Restless's first ability triggers, putting Circling Vultures from your graveyard onto the battlefield.
  Activate Bloodflow Connoisseur by sacrificing Circling Vultures.
  When Circling Vultures dies, Enduring Renewal triggers, returning Circling Vultures from your graveyard to the battlefield.
  Resolve the Bloodflow Connoisseur ability, putting a +1/+1 counter on it.
  Repeat.

  Activate Tayam by paying {3} and removing three counters from creatures you control, causing you to mill three cards and return Gaea's Cradle from your graveyard to the battlefield.
  When Gaea's Cradle enters, Mossborn Hydra triggers, doubling the number of +1/+1 counters on it.
  Repeat.
Notes: The prerequisite can be waived if Mossborn Hydra has six or more counters on it.

  Resolve the Tayam ability, causing you to mill three cards and return Hajar from your graveyard to the battlefield with a vigilance counter on it.

  Activate Overgrown Estate by sacrificing Guildless Commons.
  When Guildless Commons dies, Toph, Hardheaded Teach triggers, returning Guildless Commons from your graveyard to the battlefield tapped.
  When Guildless Commons enters, it triggers, returning the artifact cast in step 1 from the battlefield to your hand, as it's a land due to Toph, the First Metalbender.
  Resolve the Overgrown Estate ability
  Repeat.

## Combo 765-833-1059-3827
Cards: Soul of Windgrace + Waves of Aggression + Grand Warlord Radha + Guildless Commons
Color identity: BRGW | Bracket: Exhibition | Popularity: 0 | Commander: legal
Produces: Infinite combat phases; Infinite landfall triggers; Infinite magecraft triggers; Infinite self-discard triggers; Infinite storm count; Infinite untap of most creatures you control; Infinite green mana; Infinite red mana
Start: Soul of Windgrace (battlefield); Waves of Aggression (graveyard); Grand Warlord Radha (battlefield); Guildless Commons (graveyard)

Steps: Declare Grand Warlord Radha and Soul of Windgrace as attackers.

  Repeat.

## Combo 789-2399-5147--129
Cards: Colfenor, the Last Yew + Gutter Grime + Goblin Bombardment
Color identity: BRGW | Bracket: Exhibition | Popularity: 0 | Commander: legal
Produces: Infinite creature LTB; Infinite creature ETB; Infinite creature sacrifice triggers; Infinite death triggers; Infinite damage; Infinite storm count; Infinite creature tokens that are infinitely large
Requires: Creature castable for {0} (hand)
Start: Colfenor, the Last Yew (battlefield); Gutter Grime (battlefield); Goblin Bombardment (battlefield)
Prerequisites: Gutter Grime has slime counters on it at least equal to the toughness of the creature card in your hand.
Steps: Cast the creature card in your hand by paying {0}.
  Either that creature dies as a state-based action due to having zero toughness, or activate Goblin Bombardment by sacrificing that creature.

  When they attack, Klauth and Soul of Windgrace trigger.
  Resolve the Klauth trigger, adding at least nine mana that you will not lose this turn as steps and phases end.
  Resolve the Soul of Windgrace trigger, returning Selesnya Sanctuary from your graveyard to the battlefield tapped.
  When Selesnya Sanctuary enters, it triggers, returning itself from the battlefield to your hand.
  In your next main phase, cast Waves of Aggression from your graveyard for its retrace cost by paying {3}{R/W}{R/W} and discarding Selesnya Sanctuary, causing you to untap all creatures that attacked this turn, and to get an additional combat and main phase after this one.
  Repeat.

Produces: Infinite charge counters on a permanent; Infinite creature LTB; Infinite creature ETB; Infinite creature sacrifice triggers; Infinite death triggers; Infinite lifegain triggers; Infinite lifegain; Infinite creature tokens; Infinite lifeloss; Infinite card draw; Infinite draw triggers; Infinite Food tokens
Start: Peregrin Took (battlefield); Cauldron Familiar (battlefield); Spawning Pit (battlefield); Quintorius, Field Historian (battlefield)
Prerequisites: You control at least one Food.
Steps: Activate Spawning Pit's first ability by sacrificing Cauldron Familiar.
  Activate Cauldron Familiar by sacrificing a Food, returning Cauldron Familiar to the battlefield from your graveyard.
  Cauldron Familiar and Quintorius trigger.
  Resolve the Quintorius trigger, creating a token and a Food token.
  Resolve the Cauldron Familiar trigger, causing each opponent to lose 1 life and you to gain 1 life.
  Repeat

Start: Yarus, Roar of the Old Gods (battlefield); Karlov Watchdog (battlefield); Woe Strider (battlefield); Harmless Offering (hand)
Prerequisites: You control a face-down creature whose front face is a permanent card.
  You have enough mana to cast Harmless Offering.
Steps: Cast Harmless Offering by paying its mana cost, giving control of Karlov Watchdog to an opponent.
  During the opponent's turn, activate Woe Strider by sacrificing a face-down creature you control.
  When the creature dies, Yarus's second ability triggers, returning it from your graveyard to the battlefield face-down as a 2/2 creature, and then not turning it face-up due to Karlov Watchdog.
  Resolve the Woe Strider ability.
  Repeat from step 2.

## Combo 997-3234-3293-5226
Cards: Emeria Shepherd + World Shaper + Woe Strider + Lithatog
Color identity: BRGW | Bracket: Exhibition | Popularity: 0 | Commander: legal
Produces: Infinite creature LTB; Infinite creature ETB; Infinite creature sacrifice triggers; Infinite death triggers; Infinite scry 1; Infinite self-mill; Infinite landfall triggers; Return all nonland permanents from your graveyard to the battlefield; Infinitely large creature until end of turn
Start: Emeria Shepherd (battlefield); World Shaper (battlefield); Woe Strider (battlefield); Lithatog (battlefield)
Prerequisites: You control a Plains or have a Plains in your graveyard.
Steps: Activate Lithatog by sacrificing a land.
  Repeat step 1 until you control no lands.
  Activate Woe Strider by sacrificing World Shaper.
  When World Shaper dies, it triggers, return all lands from your graveyard to the battlefield tapped.
  When the lands enter, Emeria Shepherd triggers that many times.
  Resolve an Emeria Shepherd trigger that was triggered by a Plains, returning World Shaper from your graveyard to the battlefield.
  Resolve all remaining Emeria Shepherd triggers.
  Resolve the Woe Strider ability.
  Repeat
Notes: You can return all nonland permanents in your graveyard and library to the battlefield if you control there are two or more Plains between your graveyard, library and battlefield. To do this, have all triggers from Emeria Shepherd target desired permanents. Then, resolve the Plains triggers first to return those cards to the battlefield, causing all other triggers to fizzle on the stack.

## Combo 997-3263-6871--211
Cards: Toph, the First Metalbender + Woe Strider + Mycosynth Lattice
Color identity: BRGW | Bracket: Exhibition | Popularity: 0 | Commander: legal
Produces: Infinite artifact ETB; Infinite artifact LTB; Infinite artifact sacrifice triggers; Infinite creature ETB; Infinite creature LTB; Infinite creature sacrifice triggers; Infinite death triggers; Infinite landfall triggers; Infinite scry 1
Requires: Creature with ETB: Earthbend (hand)
Start: Toph, the First Metalbender (battlefield); Woe Strider (battlefield); Mycosynth Lattice (battlefield)
Prerequisites: You have enough mana to cast the earthbend creature.
Steps: Cast the earthbend creature by paying its mana cost.
  When the creature enters, it triggers, earthbending the creature, as it's an artifact due to Mycosynth Lattice, thus making it also a land due to Toph.
  Activate Woe Strider by sacrificing the earthbent creature.
  When the creature dies, it triggers, returning it from your graveyard to the battlefield tapped.
  When the creature enters, it triggers, earthbending the creature, as it's an artifact due to Mycosynth Lattice, thus making it also a land due to Toph.
  Resolve the Woe Strider ability, causing you to scry 1.
  Repeat from step 3.
Notes: Depending on the earthbend creature used, you may receive additional results. There may also be needed prerequisites that are specific for each card (such as Bumi, King of Three Trials which requires a Lesson in your graveyard).
